## 阅读常见坑（结合 ZYZ 语料）

### 1. FALSE vs NOT GIVEN

| 类型 | 信号 | 处理 |
|------|------|------|
| **FALSE** | 题干与原文**直接矛盾**（反义、数字错、对象错） | 原文能**明确反驳**题干 |
| **NOT GIVEN** | 原文**未提及**或**无法推断** | 原文没说过 / 证据不足 |

> **坑**：把「原文没写」当成 FALSE；或把「自己推理」当成原文说了。

### 2. 绝对词 & 程度词（NG/FALSE 高发）

| 词 | 风险 |
|----|------|
| all / every / always / never / only / must | 题干含绝对词 → 原文常有限定 → 易 FALSE |
| some / may / might / often / tend to / suggest | 原文弱表述 → 题干改肯定 → 易 FALSE/NG |
| the majority / mainly / largely | 部分≠全部；「大多数」≠「所有」 |

### 3. 偷换对象（rather than / instead of）

> 语料命中 **92** 篇含 **rather than**，**21** 篇含 **instead of**。
> 题干常把「A rather than B」改成「B rather than A」，或把比较对象换成近义词。

### 4. 对比 & 转折（However / Unlike / Whereas）

> **However** 出现于 **183** 篇；**Unlike** 短语 **35** 篇含 compared with/to。
> 转折后才是重点；判断题考「转折前信息」常为 FALSE。

### 5. 因果倒置（result in / result from / lead to）

> **result in** = 导致（结果）；**result from** = 源于（原因）。题干互换因果 → FALSE。

### 6. 部分正确陷阱

> 选项一半对、一半错 → 整体 FALSE；填空题同义替换勿接受「沾边义」。

### 7. 举例 ≠ 结论（for example / for instance）

> 语料 **130** 篇含 for example/instance。举例仅说明一点，不能推广为作者全部观点。

### 8. 时间 / 频率变化（used to / no longer / until）

> 原文「曾经…现在不」→ 题干说「一直…」= FALSE；**not until** 倒装句先抓时间逻辑。

## 按考频展开 · 短语

#### more … than：比……更（比较级）

> **230** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**1. P1 - Portrait of Bianca Sforza 达** — However, certain details — the subtle shading of the sitter’s face, the exquisite treatment of her hair, and the ethereal quality of the gaze — suggested a hand far more skilled than the German artist to whom it had previously been attributed.

#### It is … that …：强调句（定位主干）

> **179** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**100. P3 - What makes a musical expe** — It is evident that some children acquire skills more rapidly than others: the age of onset for walking and talking varies widely, even between children in the same household.

#### the most：最……（最高级）

> **136** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**1. P1 - A Brief History of Tea 茶叶简史** — C Tea was elevated to an art form in the Japanese tea ceremony, in which supreme importance is given to making tea in the most perfect, most polite, most graceful, most charming manner possible.

#### the same … as：与……相同（比较）

> **135** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**11. P1 - Report on a university dra** — The next step was to hold auditions in order to find six actors who felt the same way about the project as we did.

#### for instance / for example：例如（举例≠全部）

> **130** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**100. P3 - What makes a musical expe** — Someone would do this amount of practice if they practised, for example, roughly 20 hours a week for ten years.

#### according to：据……所说；根据

> **117** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**1. P1 - A Brief History of Tea 茶叶简史** — According to legend, the Emperor Shen Nung was a skilled ruler, creative scientist and patron of the arts.

#### used to：曾经（过去习惯/状态）

> **101** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**10. P1 - Maori Fish Hooks 毛利鱼钩.pdf** — Slender hooks with wide gaps were used to capture albatross and other seabirds and can be distinguished from fish hooks by their lighter construction and absence of an in-turned point.

#### rather than：而非；不是……而是（偷换对象高频）

> **92** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**10. P1 - Maori Fish Hooks 毛利鱼钩.pdf** — It also reflects how European influence contributed to, rather than completely replaced, traditional Maori skills, as native materials were replaced or complemented by metals and, more recently, artificial materials.

#### whether … or：是……还是；无论

> **87** 篇 · 级别 **S**
> 例：**1. P1 - A Brief History of Tea 茶叶简史** — Earlier traders had mentioned it, but were unclear as to whether tea should be eaten or drunk.

#### despite：尽管（让步，易与因果混淆）

> **76** 篇 · 级别 **A**
> 例：**108. P1 - Bovids 牛科动物.pdf** — Despite differences in size and appearance, bovids are united by the possession of certain common features.

#### in order to：为了

> **74** 篇 · 级别 **A**
> 例：**100. P3 - What makes a musical expe** — 33 In order to be useful, practice must be carried out regularly every day.

#### as a result：因此；结果（因果）

> **51** 篇 · 级别 **A**
> 例：**1. P1 - A Brief History of Tea 茶叶简史** — As a result, he is known as the “Father of Tea” in Japan.

#### no longer：不再（时间变化）

> **45** 篇 · 级别 **A**
> 例：**10. P1 - Maori Fish Hooks 毛利鱼钩.pdf** — However, on average, the traditional hook was usually no longer than a three-finger breadth (128 mm in length).

#### a number of：许多（数量模糊）

> **44** 篇 · 级别 **A**
> 例：**101. P3 - Yawning 打哈欠.pdf** — He also found that it is common for people to yawn a number of times in quick succession, with the yawns usually being around 31 __________ apart.

#### in addition：此外；另外

> **37** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**101. P3 - Yawning 打哈欠.pdf** — But in addition to gaping jaws, yawning has significant features that are easy to observe and analyse.

#### compared with/to：与……相比（比较考点）

> **35** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**1. P1 - Portrait of Bianca Sforza 达** — These prints, made in the paint layer before it dried, were compared with known fingerprints from Leonardo’s notebooks.

#### a variety of：各种各样的

> **35** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**108. P1 - Bovids 牛科动物.pdf** — They vary in shape and size: the relatively simple horns of a large Indian buffalo may measure around 4 m from tip to tip along the outer curve, while the various gazelles have horns with a variety of elegant curves.

#### so that：以便；所以

> **33** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**128. P1 - The Pyramid of Cestius 罗马** — One of the things that strikes you when you look at the pyramid is how steep it is, so that the shape of Cestius’ pyramid is quite unlike that of typical Egyptian ones.

#### as a result of：由于；因为

> **24** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**100. P3 - What makes a musical expe** — Studies of violin players have shown that the region of the brain responsible for controlling the movement of the left hand (the hand that requires greater precision in violin playing) increases in size as a result of practice.

#### lead to：导致

> **24** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**119. P2 - The Constant Evolution of** — 26 __________ are a problem for heirloom tomatoes because they frequently lead to damage and deterioration.

#### not only … but also：不仅……而且……

> **23** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**1. P1 - Portrait of Bianca Sforza 达** — If authentic, it would not only expand da Vinci’s already extraordinary oeuvre, but also change our understanding of his work during the late 15th century.

#### instead of：代替；而不是

> **21** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**10. P1 - Maori Fish Hooks 毛利鱼钩.pdf** — The hook’s design allowed it to catch fish by spinning away from the direction of the point and catching their jaws, instead of poking a hole through the fish or being used as a lever, as some archaeologists had suggested.

#### in terms of：就……而言；在……方面

> **21** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**104. P3 - Star Performers 明星员工.pdf** — In terms of career development, organisations invest in staff who have been selected to reach higher-level positions, while ignoring front-line employees and people with less perceived potential.

#### the majority of：大多数（部分≠全部）

> **18** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**108. P1 - Bovids 牛科动物.pdf** — Although bovids have adapted to a wide range of habitats, from arctic tundra to deep tropical forest, the majority of species favour open grassland, scrub or desert.

#### far from：远非；远未（否定强调）

> **17** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**110. P2 - Should space be explored ** — C Many of the promises of artificial intelligence are still far from being fulfilled.

#### depend on：取决于

> **17** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**104. P3 - Star Performers 明星员工.pdf** — Also, judgements about performance and ability depend on the standards used to judge what is good and bad.

#### regardless of：不管；不顾

> **15** 篇 · 级别 **B**
> 例：**11. P2 - From princes to paupers 西班** — His greatest strength as a portraitist is that regardless of the status of the sitter, be they a king and queen, the Duke of Wellington or a doctor or writer, it was the person he showed first and their position second.

#### in contrast：相比之下（对比考点）

> **14** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**123. P3 - Images and Places 风景与印记.p** — In contrast, Yamashita (2002), when focusing on local residents’ perceptions of the qualities of the water around them, noted that residents may have more difficulty expressing visual appeal than visitors, precisely because they are insiders and less conscious of aesthetic qualities.

#### refer to：指；提及；查阅

> **14** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**100. P3 - What makes a musical expe** — Are high levels of musical achievement simply the result of training and practice, or are they based on innate brain structure — what we refer to as talent?

#### contribute to：促成；加剧；贡献于

> **13** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**11. P1 - Report on a university dra** — We wanted a project in which every voice would carry equal weight and everyone would be able to contribute to every aspect of the show.

#### account for：解释；占……比例

> **11** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**117. P2 - Stress Less 工作压力.pdf** — This absence comes at a high price — while the number of insurance claims due to stress amount to only 24 ________ of the total, they account for a much higher proportion of the cost of claim payments.

#### result in：导致（结果）

> **8** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**123. P3 - Images and Places 风景与印记.p** — Furthermore, Haywood (1990) suggests that compressing the photo-taking period into a single day (as he did in his work) may result in an over-representation of tourist icons rather than ordinary places.

#### as long as：只要

> **8** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**166. P2 - Lean Production Innovatio** — Because the machinery costs so much, and is so intolerant of disruption, the mass producer keeps standard designs in production for as long as possible.

#### only when：只有当……

> **8** 篇 · 级别 **C**
> 例：**122. P1 - Footprints in the Mud 恐龙脚** — The dinosaurs, in other words, could flourish only when the competition had been knocked out.

#### in spite of：尽管

> **7** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**17. P1 - The Development of The Sil** — In spite of their secrecy about production methods, the Chinese eventually lost their monopoly on silk production.

#### to some extent：在某种程度上（程度限定）

> **6** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**102. P1 - Katherine Mansfield 新西兰作家** — Mansfield wrote in her journals of feeling isolated to some extent in New Zealand, and, in general terms, of her interest in the Maori people (New Zealand’s native people), who were often portrayed in a sympathetic light in her later stories, such as How Pearl Button Was Kidnapped.

#### play a role in：在……中起作用

> **5** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**142. P2 - Born to Trade 交易的本能.pdf** — Some researchers think that in humans, consumer products play a similar role.

#### consist of：由……组成

> **4** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**159. P1 - Scented Plants 植物的味道.pdf** — Fragrances consist of small organic particles with high vapor pressures, so that a scent compound evaporates easily if exposed to the air; chemicals which evaporate in this way are described as ‘volatile compounds’.

#### not until：直到……才（否定+倒装）

> **4** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**107. P2 - A study of western celebr** — It was not until the 1700s, when technological advances made publishing commercially viable, that the extent of a person’s fame could spread further.

#### in contrast to：与……形成对比

> **3** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**35. P3 - Nine Quandaries of Tourism** — In addition, Westerners’ interest in biodiversity stands in contrast to their indifference to the local human community.

#### as opposed to：与……相反；而非

> **2** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**165. P3 - The Placebo Effect5 安慰剂效应** — Not necessarily because you’d recommended ginseng rather than chamomile tea or used this crystal as opposed to that pressure point.

#### there is no doubt：毫无疑问（绝对语气）

> **2** 篇 · 级别 **D**

#### stem from：源于；来自

> **2** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**204. P3 - When people are ‘deaf’ to** — It has long been believed that the temporal lobes, especially the right temporal lobe, are most active when engaged in musical activity, so any musical disability should logically stem from here as well.

#### by no means：绝不（否定绝对词）

> **2** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**198. P3 - Child’s Play in Medieval ** — F However, by no means all children’s military activity was directed by adults.

#### with respect to：关于；就……而言

> **1** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**40. P3 - Five causes of collapse of** — Examples of such forces include changes in the heat put out by the sun, volcanic eruptions that inject dust into the atmosphere, changes in the orientation of the Earth’s axis with respect to its orbit, and changes in the distribution of land and ocean over the face of the Earth.

#### result from：源于（原因）

> **1** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**225. P2 - The problem of graffiti 涂** — D Localised pollution can result from some cleaning methods.

#### comprise：包含；由……组成

> **1** 篇 · 级别 **D**
> 例：**24. P2 - The International Meteorit** — Martian meteorites comprise only 0.15% of meteorites known to science—that’s a mere 61 out of 41,000 specimens.


---

## 说明

- 语料路径：`e:/BaiduNetdiskDownload/ZYZ/PDF`（ZYZ 阅读 PDF 全集）。
- 词频按**出现篇数**统计（同一 PDF 只计 1 次），比纯词频更接近「考频」。
- 可与 [`Yasi.md`](Yasi.md) 个人生词本、[`Yasi-ZXZ-阅读例句.md`](Yasi-ZXZ-阅读例句.md) 对照使用。
- 释义优先 [`Yasi.md`](Yasi.md) 个人笔记；其余为雅思阅读义项 + 在线词典补充。
- 重新生成：`python web/scripts/build-yasi-zyz-reading-freq-md.py`
